# Implementation Validation Audit

Date: 2026-06-30
Last updated: 2026-07-03

## How To Read This Audit

The current state is defined by the current release blocker, external evidence
review ledger, final publish signoff, verification evidence, remaining
validation gap, negative fixture ownership, and latest command summary sections.
The completed-in-this-pass section is chronological history and can mention
intermediate states that were later superseded, such as temporary compatibility
barrels or wrappers.

## External Evidence Review Ledger

Only branch-protection governance has been reviewed so far. Add one row per
external URL or uploaded artifact before moving any remaining QA row from
`_pending_` to `Passed:`. Every reviewed row must include a durable reviewed
artifact reference, concrete reviewer, ISO timestamp, and category-specific
proof; placeholder artifact names such as `<version>` or `<date>` do not pass
final readiness. Reviewed proof must use concrete values such as 64-hex hashes,
trusted GitHub artifact URLs, generated `versionCode`, `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT`,
and exact `Content-Range`/CORS header observations, not field-name-only notes.
Required ledger coverage spans release dry run, validation
prerequisites/device-validation bundle, APK/generated metadata, installed APK
smoke, production runtime/range, product journey manifest, Android/iOS route
rows, negative rows, and branch protection.
The current verifier enforces category coverage, so final review must also add
either separate ledger rows or a generated reviewed artifact inventory that
enumerates every concrete URL/path referenced by completed QA rows. A category
row cannot silently cover additional unreviewed URLs or artifacts. Save the
pending scaffold as
`docs/qa-evidence/<date>/external-evidence-inventory-scaffold.json` with
`npm run external-evidence:scaffold -- --inventory-path docs/qa-evidence/<date>/external-evidence-inventory-scaffold.json --qa-path docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md --packet-path <reviewed-packet.json>`,
then copy reviewed, byte-backed entries into
`docs/qa-evidence/<date>/external-evidence-inventory.json` and
verify it with
`npm run verify:external-evidence-inventory -- --inventory-path docs/qa-evidence/<date>/external-evidence-inventory.json --qa-path docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md --packet-path <reviewed-packet.json> --require-git-integrity`
before final readiness.
The final audit signoff must use that reviewed inventory path and a reviewed
device packet path; packet filenames that still contain `scaffold`,
`candidate`, `pending`, or `example` remain planning evidence only and cannot
satisfy WP-8. Readiness rejects those paths before invoking device-packet
reconciliation or external-inventory replay helpers.
For Android/iOS route and negative rows, bare `actions/runs/<id>` URLs are
supporting context only. A reviewed external artifact must include
`artifactPurpose=route-evidence` or `artifactPurpose=negative-evidence`, a route
or negative evidence manifest path, `evidenceManifestSha256=<64-hex>`,
URL-matched `sourceRunId=<digits>`, URL-matched `sourceArtifactId=<id>`,
`mediaSha256`/`fileSha256` proof, and `helperOnly=false`. Local screenshot,
recording, and archive paths in `docs/qa-evidence/...` are byte-checked by
`device-evidence:packet --check` against `mediaSha256`; URL-backed artifacts must be downloaded or
inspected, unpacked when relevant, and recorded with recomputed hashes in the
ledger or reviewed inventory.
Helper-only content such as `downloaded-release/release-evidence/**`,
`validation-prereqs.json`, bundle-verifier output, installed-smoke output, or
release-bundle helper output is denied for route/negative rows even if the
artifact is renamed; final signoff requires reviewer proof that the
route/negative manifest content is not helper-only.

## Final Publish Signoff

decision=no-publish; publishScope=android-apk-only;
iosDistributionDecision=no-publish; reviewer=_pending_; reviewedAt=_pending_;

artifactName=_pending_; runUrl=_pending_;

Final Android APK publish signoff must replace those path placeholders with
`externalEvidenceInventoryPath=docs/qa-evidence/<date>/external-evidence-inventory.json`
and `deviceEvidencePacketPath=<reviewed-packet.json>` matching the readiness
environment, plus
`stackReceiptPath=docs/qa-evidence/<date>/stack-architecture-receipt.json`, a
protected-branch candidate proof, and
`docs/qa-evidence/<date>/final-command-receipts/verification-summary.json`.
Before final readiness, write a draft signoff with deterministic final receipt
paths, run
`npm run verify:final-readiness-receipts -- --candidate-sha <payload-sha> --date <YYYY-MM-DD> --inventory-path docs/qa-evidence/<date>/external-evidence-inventory.json --packet-path <reviewed-packet.json> --stack-receipt-path docs/qa-evidence/<date>/stack-architecture-receipt.json`
commit the generated receipts with the QA/audit/evidence signoff files, then
rerun the same wrapper from that evidence signoff commit. The final
`verification-summary.json` must include protected `origin/master` ancestry
proof, passing `Last-mile GitHub release-state evidence` and `Last-mile
publish-hash variable guard` steps, and must show
`inputs.expectReadinessStatus=0`, `publishSignoffReady=true`,
`noPublishRehearsal=false`, and `status=pass`; any
`status=expected-readiness-fail` summary is no-publish rehearsal evidence only.
It must also bind `inputs.date`, `inputs.lastMileReleaseStateLivePath`, and
`inputs.lastMilePublishHashGuardLivePath` to the
`finalCommandReceiptsPath evidence date` and dated `final-command-receipts`
live paths ending in `github-release-state-prepublish-live.json` and
`publish-hash-variable-guard-prepublish-live.json`; the matching last-mile
step commands must use `--output-path` for those same live paths.
Final receipt last-mile review binding requires `inputs.repository`,
`inputs.reviewer`, and `inputs.reviewedAt` to match the `Last-mile GitHub
release-state evidence` and `Last-mile publish-hash variable guard` step
commands through `--repo`, `--reviewer`, and `--reviewed-at`;
`inputs.repository` must be the trusted Diveo repository, `inputs.reviewer`
must be a concrete reviewer, and `inputs.reviewedAt` must be an ISO timestamp
on the final evidence date. The wrapper rejects untrusted repository before
helper commands run, wrapper rejects weak reviewer before helper commands run,
and wrapper rejects stale reviewedAt before helper commands run. The static
last-mile JSON review metadata must match the final command receipt summary.
Final receipt reviewer source fail-fast binding: `inputs.reviewer` must come
from `--reviewer`, `FINAL_READINESS_REVIEWER`, `GITHUB_ACTOR`, `USERNAME`, or
`USER`; the wrapper rejects missing reviewer and wrapper rejects
final-readiness-runner before helper commands run.
Final receipt summary timing binding requires `startedAt` and `finishedAt` to
be an ISO timestamp on the final evidence date, and `finishedAt` must not be
before `startedAt`.
Final receipt summary timing binding marker: startedAt finishedAt ISO timestamp on the final evidence date finishedAt must not be before startedAt.
Final receipt input fail-fast binding: `inputs.date` must be the current UTC
capture date; the wrapper rejects stale date and wrapper rejects future date
before helper commands run. `inputs.qaPath` must be `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md`;
the wrapper rejects noncanonical QA path before helper commands run.
Final receipt evidence-dir fail-fast binding: `inputs.evidenceDir` must be `docs/qa-evidence/<date>/final-command-receipts` and match the finalCommandReceiptsPath directory; the wrapper rejects noncanonical evidence-dir before helper commands run.
Final receipt inventory fail-fast binding: `inputs.inventoryPath` must be `docs/qa-evidence/<date>/external-evidence-inventory.json`; the wrapper rejects noncanonical inventory path before helper commands run.
Final receipt runUrl repository binding requires `inputs.repository` to match
the audit runUrl repository so final receipts and release evidence use the same
trusted Diveo repository.
Final receipt runUrl repository binding marker: inputs.repository audit runUrl repository same trusted Diveo repository.
Final receipt release-state query repository binding requires release-state
`commands` for `workflows`, `releaseRuns`, `workflowDispatchRuns`, and
`releases` to use `gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/actions/workflows` or
`gh run/release list` with `-R` or `--repo` matching `inputs.repository`, the
final command receipt summary repository.
Final receipt release-state query repository binding marker: commands workflows releaseRuns workflowDispatchRuns releases inputs.repository final command receipt summary repository gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/actions/workflows -R --repo.
Final receipt live last-mile JSON binding requires
`inputs.lastMileReleaseStateLivePath` and
`inputs.lastMilePublishHashGuardLivePath` to exist as valid JSON with
`status=pass`, `ok=true`, matching repository, reviewer, `reviewedAt`,
`checkedAt` on the final evidence date, and `releaseReadinessImpact.status=ready`.
Final receipt live last-mile JSON binding marker: inputs.lastMileReleaseStateLivePath inputs.lastMilePublishHashGuardLivePath valid JSON status=pass ok=true repository reviewer reviewedAt checkedAt releaseReadinessImpact.status=ready.
Final receipt live output creation binding: `Last-mile GitHub release-state evidence`
and `Last-mile publish-hash variable guard` must create valid JSON at
`inputs.lastMileReleaseStateLivePath` and
`inputs.lastMilePublishHashGuardLivePath`; the wrapper removes stale live output
before each capture step, validates repository, reviewer, `reviewedAt`, and
`checkedAt` on the final evidence date, then requires `status=pass`, `ok=true`,
and `releaseReadinessImpact.status=ready` for every live step with
`expectedStatus=0`; it must stop before `Final release readiness` and record
`outputProblems` if either file is missing, invalid, mismatched, or not ready.
Final receipt publish-hash query repository binding requires every
`variableQueries[].command` for `EXPECTED_RELEASE_APK_SHA256` and
`EXPECTED_RELEASE_PUBLISH_IDENTITY_SHA256` to include `--repo` matching
`inputs.repository`, the final command receipt summary repository.
Final receipt publish-hash query repository binding marker: variableQueries[].command --repo inputs.repository final command receipt summary repository EXPECTED_RELEASE_APK_SHA256 EXPECTED_RELEASE_PUBLISH_IDENTITY_SHA256.
Final receipt step-log byte replay requires every final summary step to include
`stdoutPath`, `stderrPath`, `stdoutSha256`, `stderrSha256`, and
`combinedSha256`; readiness reopens the `final-command-receipts` logs and
verifies byte replay before publish signoff can pass.
Final receipt wrapper step set requires all 11 final receipt wrapper steps in
order: Documentation drift audit; Final readiness focused tests; External
evidence inventory replay; Device packet reconciliation; Strict handoff receipt
replay; Stack architecture receipt replay; Protected master ref refresh;
Protected candidate ancestry proof; Last-mile GitHub release-state evidence;
Last-mile publish-hash variable guard; Final release readiness. The wrapper
rejects missing, duplicate, or reordered recorded step evidence before
status=pass.
Final receipt wrapper step set marker: all 11 final receipt wrapper steps
Documentation drift audit Final readiness focused tests External evidence
inventory replay Device packet reconciliation Strict handoff receipt replay
Stack architecture receipt replay Protected master ref refresh Protected
candidate ancestry proof Last-mile GitHub release-state evidence Last-mile
publish-hash variable guard Final release readiness wrapper rejects missing
duplicate reordered recorded step evidence before status=pass.
Final receipt wrapper step status requires every final wrapper step to record
`expectedStatus=0` and `status=0` before publish signoff.
Final release readiness command/env binding requires
`verify-release-readiness.js --strict-final-inputs` to run with
`EXTERNAL_EVIDENCE_INVENTORY_PATH`, `DEVICE_EVIDENCE_PACKET_PATH`,
`RELEASE_CANDIDATE_SHA`, and `FINAL_READINESS_RECEIPT_BOOTSTRAP` matching
`finalCommandReceiptsPath`; no other env values are allowed because these are
the safe final input keys.
Final receipt internal command binding requires `Final readiness focused tests`
to run `node_modules/vitest/vitest.mjs run` with
`scripts/verify-release-readiness.test.mjs`, `scripts/verify-doc-drift.test.mjs`,
`scripts/verify-external-evidence-inventory.test.mjs`,
`scripts/device-evidence-packet.test.mjs`,

`scripts/stack-architecture-receipt.test.mjs`; `inputs.qaPath` must be
`docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md`; inventory replay and device packet steps must use
`--inventory-path`, `--qa-path`, `--packet-path`, `--candidate-sha`, and
`--require-git-integrity`; stack receipt replay must run
`scripts/stack-architecture-receipt.js --require-assets --verify docs/qa-evidence/<date>/stack-architecture-receipt.json`;
protected proof steps must run
`git fetch --no-tags origin master` and `git merge-base --is-ancestor`.
publish hash repository variables while decision=no-publish:
EXPECTED_RELEASE_APK_SHA256=absent; EXPECTED_RELEASE_PUBLISH_IDENTITY_SHA256=absent.
Final publish uses workflow_dispatch inputs `expected_apk_sha256` and
`expected_publish_identity_sha256` from the reviewed dry-run artifact, not
push-time repository variables.
branchProtectionReady=true; protected branch=master; required check=quality / quality; branchProtectionEvidence=docs/qa-evidence/2026-07-01/master-branch-protection.json.
lastMileReleaseStateEvidence=_pending_; lastMilePublishHashGuardEvidence=_pending_.
Immediately before any `workflow_dispatch publish_release=true`, rerun
`release-evidence:github-release-state` and `release-evidence:publish-hash-guard`
and record
`lastMileReleaseStateEvidence=docs/qa-evidence/<date>/github-release-state-prepublish.json`
plus
`lastMilePublishHashGuardEvidence=docs/qa-evidence/<date>/publish-hash-variable-guard-prepublish.json`
here. The release-state JSON must show `status=pass`, concrete reviewer
metadata on the final evidence date, passing query summaries, zero GitHub
releases, `releaseReadinessImpact.status=ready`, and a successful
`workflow_dispatch` dry run whose URL matches `runUrl`; the publish-hash guard
JSON must show concrete reviewer metadata, `releaseReadinessImpact.status=ready`,
GitHub CLI query provenance, and `EXPECTED_RELEASE_APK_SHA256` plus
`EXPECTED_RELEASE_PUBLISH_IDENTITY_SHA256` absent. The final wrapper and publish
workflow also capture live prepublish guard JSON under `final-command-receipts/`
and `release-evidence/` before readiness/release creation.
Final receipt pass-summary contradiction binding: final command receipt summary
with `status=pass` and `ok=true` must not include `failedStep` or non-empty
`outputProblems`.
Final receipt pass-summary contradiction binding marker: final command receipt summary status=pass ok=true must not include failedStep or non-empty outputProblems.

## Completed In This Pass

- Hardened `scripts/run-final-readiness-receipts.js` so the final wrapper
  validates its recorded step evidence before writing a pass summary. The
  wrapper now rejects missing, duplicate, or reordered final receipt steps at
  the source, and `scripts/run-final-readiness-receipts.test.mjs` covers the
  missing-step regression. Docs drift now requires this source-level step-set
  guard in the QA runbook, implementation plan, and audit.
- Hardened bridge package provenance as part of the web/native release
  contract. `scripts/write-release-evidence-summary.js` now writes
  `gsavPackageProvenance` for `@opsiclear/gsav-bridge` and
  `@opsiclear/gsav-client`, including the `file:vendor/...` specifier and
  64-hex vendored tarball SHA. The bundle verifier rejects missing, malformed,
  drifted, or tampered package provenance; release-readiness rejects dry-run
  rows that omit those bridge/client details; and docs drift keeps the QA guide,
  implementation plan, and audit templates aligned.
- Strengthened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so a completed release
  dry-run row must include explicit `publishArtifactIdentitySha256=<64-hex>`
  evidence, not just checksum-manifest evidence. The QA guide and
  implementation plan now use `publishArtifactIdentitySha256=<64-hex sha>` in
  their copyable dry-run row templates, and `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js`
  rejects future dry-run template drift.
- Strengthened `scripts/write-release-evidence-summary.js` so summary
  generation fails before writing `dry-run-summary.json` unless
  `release-evidence/release-candidate.txt` includes `releaseCandidateSha`,
  `appVersion`, `packageVersion`, and `androidVersionCode`; the candidate SHA is
  a commit SHA matching `RELEASE_CANDIDATE_SHA`, app/package versions are semver
  values matching `releaseVersion`, and `androidVersionCode` is numeric. This
  prevents a stable `publishArtifactIdentitySha256` from being calculated over
  missing, malformed, or drifted candidate identity fields.
- Added production safeguards for QA-only validation triggers:
  `EXPO_PUBLIC_GSAV_QA_CONTROLS` and
  `EXPO_PUBLIC_GSAV_QA_AUTH_DELAY_MS` are allowed for device negative QA, but
  `scripts/verify-native-production-config.js` now rejects any production build
  where either value is set to anything except unset or `0` in direct env or the
  EAS production profile. It also rejects release workflow source that mentions
  those flags, so a later build, prebuild, or verification step cannot inject
  them after config verification. The release-candidate bundle now forces
  placeholder production checks to report `releaseWorkflowForbidsQaFlags=true`,
  `qaControlsDisabled=true`, and `qaAuthDelayDisabled=true`.
- Added `npm run verify:workflows` and wired it into `verify:local`. The new
  verifier parses every `.github/workflows/*.yml` file and checks the project
  workflow contract: CI calls reusable quality, quality exposes
  `workflow_call`, the quality job runs `npm run verify:local` plus the
  placeholder production-config audit, release has the required dispatch
  inputs and production evidence commands, release evidence is uploaded, and
  release workflow source does not mention QA-only env flags.
- Closed the three-agent architecture follow-ups without changing the hybrid
  direction: React Native remains the mobile product shell, while hosted
  gsav-hosting owns decode/render/playback. The remaining blockers are external
  device, artifact, production host, range-probe, and release dry-run evidence.
- Added platform-aware GSAV web URL defaults: Android development builds use
  `http://10.0.2.2:5191`, other development targets use
  `http://127.0.0.1:5191`, and production still fails closed unless
  `EXPO_PUBLIC_GSAV_WEB_URL` is configured.
- Hardened configured GSAV web URLs so malformed or non-HTTP(S) values return
  the same not-configured path instead of crashing during embed URL construction.
- Kept `shared/gsavWeb.ts` platform-agnostic after focused tests exposed that a
  direct `react-native` import in shared code makes Vitest parse React Native's
  Flow source. `features/player/GsavWebView.tsx` now passes `Platform.OS` at the
  native boundary.
- Tightened GSAV shell-route matching to first path segment only, so
  `/watchlist`, `/explore-preview`, `/gsavish`, and `/gsav-diagnostics-old` no
  longer match the embedded GSAV shell rule.
- Rejected protocol-relative native embed paths such as `//evil.example/watch`
  so player-owned URL construction cannot escape the configured GSAV origin.
- Promoted `.env.example` into the active docs-drift set and replaced stale
  "World B native browse" wording with current native catalog
  browse/search/creator configuration language.
- Added unexpired ISO `expires` metadata to every sibling-feature allowlist entry
  and updated the implementation-plan ledger so future exceptions must carry
  owner, `reason`, exit criteria, expiry, and focused allow/reject tests.
- Tightened docs-drift tests so the active checked-file set must contain all 10
  active files, including `.env.example`.
- Hardened production range CORS across `npm run gsav:preflight`,
  `npm run verify:release-evidence-bundle`, and
  `npm run verify:release-readiness`: publish-counted range evidence must show
  `Access-Control-Allow-Origin=*` or the production GSAV origin and concrete
  `Access-Control-Expose-Headers` set to `Accept-Ranges, Content-Length,
  Content-Range, ETag`; wrong-origin allow-origin values and negated
  exposed-header prose now fail.
- Updated strict production range probes to send `Origin: <GSAV web origin>` so
  CORS validation matches browser-style origin-reflecting host behavior.
- Hardened release-readiness range URL checks so all `127/8` loopback hosts are
  rejected as local/private production `.gsav` evidence, not only `127.0.0.1`.
- Added release bundle validation that `dry-run-summary.rangeProbeUrl` and
  `rangeRequest` match `gsav-preflight.json` `rangeAsset.url` and
  `requestRange`, so a dry-run cannot summarize a different `.gsav` asset than
  the one preflight actually probed.
- Required release dry-run readiness rows to state that
  `dry-run-summary.rangeProbeUrl` and `dry-run-summary.rangeRequest` matched the
  corresponding `gsav-preflight.json` range asset fields.
- Added docs-drift markers for the browser-readable range CORS contract and
  synced the QA evidence fields, implementation-plan work packages, WP-6
  runbook, and current release blocker table with the stricter header proof.
- Hardened `npm run gsav:preflight` so production-required range probes fail
  unless `Content-Range` is exact `bytes 0-0/<size>`, while optional local
  probes still record `contentRangeExact=false` for Vite preview behavior.
- Added a production-config source/workflow guard for
  `preflightRequiresExactRangeContentRange` and `verifiesRangeProbePreflight`,
  then covered both the positive and removal cases in focused tests.
- Fixed evidence metadata on Windows/npm-run shells by falling back to
  `npm_config_user_agent` when `npm.cmd --version` is unavailable.
- Refreshed local GSAV preview validation evidence through direct Vite preview:
  Diveo preflight/runtime smoke passed with npm metadata and local host identity,
  while default gsav-hosting smoke exposed a remote `elly` CORS-header gap.
- Hardened publish-counted runtime-smoke host identity parsing so completed
  rows must include an explicit deployed value such as
  `gsavHostingCommit=<sha/build>` or `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT=<sha/build>`.
  Vague or placeholder wording such as "host identity present", "configured",
  or "unavailable" no longer satisfies the row.
- Added focused readiness coverage for explicit runtime host identity values,
  then aligned the QA template, implementation plan, audit blocker table, and
  shell architecture wording with the React Native/web ownership contract.
- Clarified the elegant structure boundary: React Native owns product shell,
  native navigation, browse, search, library, auth, saved/follow, and settings;
  `../gsav-hosting/apps/web` owns GSAV decode/render/playback/runtime chrome;
  `/explore` remains the documented hosted-runtime browse exception.
- Hardened runtime-smoke readiness so a scoped exception no longer satisfies
  publish readiness. Runtime-smoke exceptions can document a blocker, but the
  final gate now fails until real non-local HTTPS production smoke with explicit
  deployed host identity lands.
- Hardened dry-run host identity metadata checks so
  `GSAV_HOST_IDENTITY_URL` and `hostIdentity.url` must be matching HTTPS
  non-local URLs, and expected/observed identities must still match
  `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT`.
- Hardened range and no-publish proof validation: preflight range evidence must
  be exact `Content-Range: bytes 0-0/<decimal-size>`, and dry-run
  no-publish evidence must include authenticated `gh release view` not-found
  proof through `githubReleaseLookup=not_found`; auth/API/network failures are
  inconclusive and fail the dry-run gate.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so completed Release APK
  artifact rows must include explicit 64-hex `apkSha256` and
  `manifestSha256` values. Generic `apkSha256 checksum recorded` and
  `manifestSha256 checksum recorded` wording no longer satisfies the artifact
  row.
- Hardened installed-release APK smoke readiness so completed rows must include
  explicit 64-hex `apkSha256`, `apkSha256MatchesDryRunSummary=true`,
  `installedVersionCode=<app.json versionCode>`, and
  `installedVersionCodeMatchesDryRunSummary=true`. Generic "matched dry-run
  summary" wording and installed `versionCode` drift now fail.
- Added readiness regressions for weak APK/manifest checksum text, weak
  installed APK checksum text, non-explicit dry-run-summary match fields, and
  installed versionCode mismatch, then updated QA and implementation-plan
  templates with the stricter artifact evidence contract.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so publish-counted
  JS runtime-smoke rows must include concrete `GSAV_BRIDGE_READY` version and
  `minVersion` evidence. A generic bridge-compatible phrase or `bridge v1`
  without `minVersion` no longer satisfies readiness.
- Hardened production range-probe validation so completed rows must include a
  real `Content-Range: bytes 0-0/<size>` response value, not only the
  `Content-Range` keyword.
- Hardened release artifact, generated-version, and release dry-run rows so
  checksum-manifest evidence must include an explicit 64-hex SHA256 value.
  Generic `sha256 checksum` wording no longer satisfies the row contract.
- Added focused readiness regressions for missing runtime bridge `minVersion`,
  missing concrete range `Content-Range`, and weak checksum-manifest SHA
  values, then aligned `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` and
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` with the stricter evidence
  contract.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so completed release dry-run
  QA rows must mention `gh release view v<version>` or equivalent command
  evidence proving the release tag was absent, in addition to
  `githubReleasePresent=false`. This keeps the row-level summary aligned with
  the uploaded `release-evidence/no-publish-side-effect.txt` proof.
- Added readiness coverage for missing `gh release view` text and updated the
  QA and implementation-plan dry-run templates to require that command proof.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so completed release dry-run
  QA rows must summarize the semantic no-publish proof fields from
  `release-evidence/no-publish-side-effect.txt`: `githubReleasePresent=false`,
  git status limited to generated `release-evidence/**` outputs,
  `git rev-parse HEAD`, remote ref HEAD, and `workflowSha` matching. A broad
  "no release or push" statement is no longer enough for the final readiness
  row.
- Added readiness coverage for missing no-publish proof row details and aligned
  the implementation-plan dry-run template with the stricter row contract.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so release dry-run QA rows
  must include explicit, comparable host identity values:
  `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT=<actual build/commit>`,
  `hostIdentity.expectedIdentity=<actual expected identity>`, and
  `hostIdentity.observedIdentity=<actual host-served identity>`. Generic row
  text such as `hostIdentity.expectedIdentity=GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT` or
  "observed identity matched" no longer satisfies publish readiness.
- Added readiness regressions for generic dry-run host identity wording and
  mismatched observed identity values, then updated the QA and implementation
  plan dry-run templates to require concrete expected and observed values.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-evidence-bundle.js` so dry-run
  `release-evidence/no-publish-side-effect.txt` proof is semantic rather than
  marker-only: non-evidence `git status --short` entries now fail, and
  `git rev-parse HEAD` plus the remote ref must match `workflowSha`.
- Added bundle-verifier regressions for dirty non-evidence status, stale local
  HEAD, and stale remote ref proof.
- Tightened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so a release dry-run row must
  name `release-evidence/no-publish-side-effect.txt`,
  `hostIdentity.url`, and `hostIdentity.expectedIdentity`, in addition to
  `GSAV_HOST_IDENTITY_URL`, `hostIdentityVerified=true`, and observed identity
  matching `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT`.
- Tightened `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so active QA, implementation-plan,
  and audit docs must keep `hostIdentity.url` and
  `hostIdentity.expectedIdentity` in the release dry-run contract.
- Hardened `scripts/gsav-native-preflight.js` so production preflight can fetch
  `GSAV_HOST_IDENTITY_URL`, require
  `GSAV_NATIVE_PREFLIGHT_REQUIRE_HOST_IDENTITY=1`, emit
  `hostIdentityVerified=true`, and reject host identity prefixes shorter than
  seven characters.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-native-production-config.js` so the release
  workflow must set `GSAV_HOST_IDENTITY_URL`, `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT`, and
  `GSAV_NATIVE_PREFLIGHT_REQUIRE_HOST_IDENTITY: "1"` in the actual
  `npm run gsav:preflight` step.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-evidence-bundle.js`,
  `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js`, and `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so
  dry-run evidence must prove `hostIdentityVerified=true`,
  `hostIdentity.url`, `hostIdentity.expectedIdentity`, and
  `hostIdentity.observedIdentity` matching `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT`.
- Hardened `scripts/capture-android-installed-smoke.js` so installed-release
  APK smoke evidence records `apkSha256`, optional
  `release-evidence/dry-run-summary.json` identity, `releaseCandidateSha`,
  workflow run/artifact identity, `dumpsys package` installed `versionCode`,
  and whether the APK SHA and installed `versionCode` match the dry-run
  summary. When a dry-run summary is supplied, the helper fails before `adb`
  runs if the APK SHA does not match.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so a completed
  Release-installed APK smoke row must include exact-artifact proof:
  `apkSha256`, dry-run summary matching, `dumpsys package` or installed
  `versionCode`, `installedVersionCodeMatchesDryRunSummary`, release-candidate
  identity, and `--ci-artifact-url`/`ciArtifactUrl`, in addition to package,
  deep-link, hidden-chrome, bridge/error, retry, resume, and filtered logcat
  evidence.
- Updated `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` and
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` with the stricter installed APK
  smoke command, result template, and evidence requirements. Publish-counted
  installed-smoke evidence now needs `--dry-run-summary-path
  release-evidence/dry-run-summary.json` and an inspected CI artifact URL.
- Ran three read-only agent reviews across architecture elegance, verification
  gates, and release/device validation. The reviews agreed that the current
  hybrid is the elegant target: React Native owns the product shell and
  gsav-hosting owns decode/render/playback/runtime chrome. Remaining work is
  evidence closure, exact fixture ownership, and release validation rather than
  another architecture pivot.
- Updated `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` so route ownership and
  publish-gate route scope are separate. `/login` and `/settings` remain native
  product routes, but they are not publish-readiness route rows unless their
  behavior affects player session, data saver, retry, resume, or another
  publish-gate signal.
- Added an active protocol import ownership note to the implementation plan:
  `@opsiclear/gsav-bridge` remains player/bridge-owned,
  `@opsiclear/gsav-client` remains behind documented service or feature
  adapters, and `scripts/verify-import-boundaries.js` is the sibling-feature
  exception ledger of record with the 14-entry ratchet ceiling.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-evidence-bundle.js` so production dry-run
  bundles must include the same five preflight route URLs required by QA and
  release-readiness evidence: `/explore?embed=native&dataSaver=1`,
  `/native-diagnostics?embed=native`, `/watch/test?embed=native`,
  `/watch/test?t=2.5&embed=native`, and `/watch/elly?embed=native`.
- Added `scripts/verify-release-evidence-bundle.test.mjs` regressions for
  missing required preflight route coverage, missing dry-run no-publish proof,
  and a dry-run proof that shows an existing GitHub release.
- Updated `.github/workflows/release.yml` so non-publishing
  `workflow_dispatch` runs write `release-evidence/no-publish-side-effect.txt`
  before `dry-run-summary.json`. The proof records `publishRelease=false`,
  `git status --short`, `git rev-parse HEAD`, the remote ref, `gh release view
  v<version>`, and `githubReleasePresent=false`; the evidence-bundle verifier
  now requires those markers in dry-run mode.
- Updated `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` and
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` dry-run requirements and templates
  so release owners record `no-publish-side-effect.txt`, all five preflight
  routes, `GSAV_HOST_IDENTITY_URL`, `hostIdentityVerified=true`, and
  `hostIdentity.observedIdentity` matching `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT`.
- Rewrote the active release-candidate checklist to use
  `npm run verify:release-candidate` as the canonical local bundle, then list
  the separate artifact, installed APK smoke, production dry-run,
  release-evidence bundle, no-publish proof, and final readiness gates. The
  checklist now states that local `gsav:preflight` and
  `gsav:runtime-smoke` are pre-release confidence only, and that releases on or
  after `2026-08-01` must renew or fix the accepted dependency-audit
  disposition before the candidate bundle can count.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so the release dry-run row
  must summarize production preflight coverage for
  `/explore?embed=native&dataSaver=1`, `/native-diagnostics?embed=native`,
  `/watch/test?embed=native`, `/watch/test?t=2.5&embed=native`, and
  `/watch/elly?embed=native`. This keeps production host preflight tied to the
  required native embed route matrix even though it is represented inside the
  dry-run row rather than as a separate readiness row.
- Updated `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` and
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` dry-run requirements and templates
  so release owners record the enforced route matrix in row text.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so active `npm run ...` references in
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` and
  `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_VALIDATION_AUDIT.md` are checked against
  `package.json`. The implementation plan check covers the active runbook and
  release gates while ignoring historical phase notes; the audit check covers
  current blockers, latest command summary, remaining validation gaps, and
  negative fixture ownership while ignoring completed-history notes.
- Added docs-drift regressions proving missing active implementation-plan and
  audit scripts fail, while historical deleted-script notes remain allowed.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so a completed QA evidence
  `Result` must start with `Passed:` or `Passed with scoped exception:`. The
  readiness gate now rejects non-canonical prefixes such as `Verified:` or
  `Complete:` and rejects failure, skip, pending, or blocker status language
  inside completed result text while still allowing observed signals such as
  cross-origin navigation being blocked.
- Added focused release-readiness tests for accepted canonical result prefixes,
  rejected non-canonical prefixes, rejected skip/failure wording, and the
  generated-version row using `Passed: versionCode ...` instead of the older
  `Passed with versionCode:` phrasing.
- Updated `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` and
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` so contributors use the enforced
  result grammar in evidence rows and keep `_pending_` plus an audit blocker
  until a row can truthfully use one of the accepted `Passed` prefixes.
- Updated `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` after three read-only agent
  reviews to make the active work packages the clear source of truth, demote
  the release-candidate checklist to a WP-4-through-WP-8 runbook view, and
  state that `npm run verify:release-candidate` is a pre-release implementation
  gate rather than publish readiness.
- Added a role-assignment placeholder table and a negative fixture manifest to
  the implementation plan. The manifest names owner/repo, trigger, expected
  signal, and ready/blocked status for missing host config, offline retry,
  cross-origin navigation, unsupported renderer, auth initialization delay, and
  ended playback before device QA can close those rows.
- Split WP-4 device validation into local rehearsal versus publish-counted
  validation, clarified WP-5 local `docs/qa-evidence/...` evidence versus CI
  uploaded `release-evidence/...` outputs, and made WP-8 require human
  inspection or bundle-verifier checking of linked GitHub artifacts before
  signoff.
- Aligned `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` and ADR 0002 with the stricter runtime-smoke
  semantics: production runtime smoke is publish evidence, while a scoped
  runtime-smoke exception records a no-publish blocker until real production
  smoke evidence lands or the audit records an explicit no-publish decision.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so active evidence-contract docs reject
  stale generic evidence URL wording that does not name the trusted repositories.
  Current QA, implementation-plan, and audit sections must keep the trusted
  GitHub CI/artifact/release URL rule tied to `opsiclear/diveo` or
  `opsiclear/gsav-hosting`; historical audit notes can still preserve older
  wording outside the current sections.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so ADR 0002 must keep the
  runtime-plus-native-catalog/product-surface supersession scope, and the shell
  architecture guide must keep Android emulator `10.0.2.2` plus physical-device
  host LAN URL guidance. Focused docs-drift tests cover both regressions.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so the current release blocker table
  must keep each concrete blocker category visible: Android, iOS, negative
  fixtures, production runtime smoke, range probe, APK/install smoke, generated
  version metadata, release dry run, and publish readiness.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so every comma- or
  semicolon-separated Evidence path entry must independently be either an
  existing `docs/qa-evidence/...` file or a trusted GitHub CI/artifact/release
  URL under `opsiclear/diveo` or `opsiclear/gsav-hosting`. Mixed cells now fail
  on unsupported labels, freeform notes, drive paths, `file://` URLs, and
  non-QA local paths instead of passing because one entry is valid.
- Added focused unit and wiring coverage for unsupported mixed evidence entries:
  valid local evidence plus a note, trusted URL plus `C:/tmp/...`,
  `docs/not-qa/...`, `file:///...`, `./docs/qa-evidence/...`, markdown links
  with valid targets, and an otherwise complete readiness table with one stray
  evidence entry.
- Aligned the implementation plan, QA runbook, shell architecture doc, ADR 0002,
  and this audit with the enforced evidence grammar and frozen-payload model.
  The current blocker table now lists production JS runtime smoke separately,
  ADR 0002 explicitly supersedes catalog/product-surface ownership from ADR
  0001, and Android emulator validation now calls out `10.0.2.2` instead of the
  host-side `127.0.0.1` fallback.
- Ran three read-only agent reviews for architecture structure, release
  verification, and documentation/runbook consistency. The converged structure
  keeps React Native as the product shell, `../gsav-hosting/apps/web` as the
  decode/render/playback runtime, `/explore` as the only hosted browse
  exception, WP-1 as a structural ratchet, and WP-7 as maintenance-only until
  real device/artifact/release evidence lands.
- Added superseded-doc drift guardrails so ADR 0001 and root historical planning
  docs must point to the current implementation plan, QA runbook, or audit
  before docs drift can pass. Added banners to `docs/adr/0001-gsav-pivot.md`,
  `IMPROVEMENT_PLAN.md`, `IMPROVEMENT_CHECKLIST.md`, and
  `REMAINING_GAPS_PLAN.md`.
- Extended `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` output with
  `checkedSupersededFiles` so CI/audit logs show which historical documents
  were checked for superseded banners, not only the nine active docs.
- Hardened release-readiness evidence URL validation so generic HTTPS links,
  artifact-looking URLs from arbitrary hosts, and GitHub links outside
  `opsiclear/diveo` or `opsiclear/gsav-hosting` no longer count as publish
  evidence. Local evidence still must be an existing `docs/qa-evidence/...`
  file.
- Added a 28-row evidence closure board to
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` so each release-readiness row has a
  command or action, durable artifact, and current blocker. The plan now also
  states that `/login` and `/settings` are native product surfaces but are not
  release-readiness route rows for this publish gate unless the route matrix is
  expanded.
- Aligned `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` with the stricter verifier: installed APK
  smoke evidence now requires package name, exact `gsav://explore`,
  `gsav://gsav-diagnostics`, and `gsav://watch/test` deep links, plus filtered
  React Native/Chromium/WebView logs; release APK artifact and generated
  version metadata templates now include release-candidate SHA,
  evidence-signoff SHA, workflow run URL, artifact name, and checksum-manifest
  SHA.
- Strengthened `scripts/verify-release-evidence-bundle.js` so
  `signoff-diff-files.txt` is parsed, must include `payloadCandidateRef`,
  `payloadCandidateSha`, and `evidenceSignoffSha`, must match the payload and
  signoff identities in `dry-run-summary.json`, and must list only
  QA/audit/evidence files. Focused tests now cover allowed signoff diffs,
  missing identity fields, SHA drift, and disallowed source/package diff
  entries.
- Strengthened `scripts/verify-native-production-config.js` so the
  release-candidate config gate also audits the
  `scripts/verify-release-evidence-bundle.js` source for signoff-diff parser,
  payload/signoff identity, and allowed changed-file validation markers.
- Deleted the broad `features/player/index.ts` helper barrel, moved
  `features/player/bridge.test.ts` to explicit leaf-module imports, and added an
  import-boundary rule so reintroducing the player helper barrel fails the
  architecture gate.
- Moved saved-scene backend-ID lookup from `features/scene/` into
  `features/social/loadSavedScenesByBackendIds.ts`, keeping `features/scene`
  focused on presentation, scene types, and sharing. The social-to-scene
  allowlist now excludes the removed lookup path and focused tests reject it.
- Removed the `scene -> preferences/useTheme` sibling dependency. `SceneCard`
  now receives theme colors from catalog and social callers, and the
  import-boundary budget ratcheted from 15 to 14 entries with a focused reject
  test preventing shared scene presentation from reading app theme state
  directly.
- Separated negative fixture readiness into its own current release blocker so
  cross-origin navigation, unsupported renderer, auth initialization delay, and
  ended playback cannot be hidden inside general Android/iOS route QA. These
  rows remain owner-blocked until the named fixtures, flags, or hosted controls
  exist and distinct Android/iOS evidence can be captured.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so the QA negative fixture
  inventory must name concrete trigger, expected-signal, and fixture-status
  details for every release-gated negative case. Placeholder text such as
  `Trigger for ...`, weak expected-signal text, or owner-blocked statuses that
  do not name the missing fixture, flag, route, or control now fail readiness
  before device evidence can be accepted.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so owner-blocked QA negative-fixture
  inventory rows must have matching backlog rows with concrete target owner,
  required fixture shape, and acceptance criteria. Stale backlog rows for
  non-blocked or removed cases now fail docs drift before validation work can
  drift away from the QA runbook.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so completed release dry-run
  evidence rows must name the required uploaded `release-evidence/` files,
  signoff diff QA/audit/evidence-only proof, preflight/runtime commit-match
  proof, and concrete GSAV host identity instead of satisfying publish
  readiness with a generic `release-evidence/**` note.
- Hardened the release dry-run commit-match check again so generic wording such
  as "the workflow commit matched" is not enough. Completed dry-run rows now
  must state that both `release-evidence/gsav-preflight.json` and
  `release-evidence/gsav-runtime-smoke.json` recorded `diveoCommit` matching
  `releaseCandidateSha`.
- Hardened the release dry-run detail checks so completed rows must also name
  `candidate_ref`, `evidenceSignoffSha`, release evidence bundle verifier
  proof, and explicit no commit/no push/no version-bump side-effect proof.
  Generic "no publish side effect" wording no longer satisfies those separate
  evidence requirements.
- Hardened release APK artifact and generated-version readiness checks so those
  rows must include release-candidate SHA, evidence signoff SHA, artifact name,
  checksum-manifest SHA, and workflow run URL. This aligns the final gate with
  the QA required evidence-fields table instead of relying only on APK and
  versionCode keywords.
- Hardened installed-release APK smoke readiness checks so completed rows must
  include the `npm run android:installed-smoke` command, `--apk-path`,
  `--output-path`, Android OS version, and Android WebView version in addition
  to the previously required route, hidden-chrome, bridge/error, retry, and
  resume signals.
- Added a more detailed remaining implementation plan that freezes the hybrid
  architecture before device QA, separates local verification from native/
  artifact/release validation, calls out the allowlist ratchet, and records the
  signoff boundary between `verify:release-evidence-bundle`, release workflow
  diff checks, and `verify:release-readiness`.
- Corrected combined Android/iOS negative-evidence examples so local evidence
  entries start with `docs/qa-evidence/...`, matching the
  `verify:release-readiness` parser.
- Accepted the native-product plus embedded-web-player architecture in
  `docs/adr/0002-native-app-with-web-player.md`.
- Updated README, contributing, architecture, QA, and agent guidance to match
  the current ownership model.
- Changed native WebView and Expo web iframe embeds to use
  `buildNativeEmbedUrl(..., { dataSaver })`, which enforces `embed=native` and
  composes `dataSaver=1`.
- Extended `npm run gsav:preflight` to cover `/explore?embed=native&dataSaver=1`.
- Fixed Library loading so saved scenes resolve by saved backend IDs instead of
  intersecting with the first feed page.
- Removed obsolete `World A` / `World B` shorthand from active code comments.
- Split player URL, route, navigation gate, session, bridge type, and reducer
  logic into `features/player/*`; `utils/gsavBridge.ts` remains a compatibility
  barrel.
- Moved the native WebView host, Expo web iframe host, and shared watch screen
  into `features/player/`; temporary `components/GsavWebView*.tsx` and
  `components/GsavScreen.tsx` compatibility wrappers were later removed.
- Updated `/watch/:id`, `/gsav/:id`, `/explore`, and `/gsav-diagnostics` route
  files to use the feature-owned player implementation.
- Added settings hydration and auth-initialization gates before embedded player
  mount/session injection.
- Added inbound WebView bridge origin validation where the native event reports
  a page URL.
- Tightened `GSAV_PLAYBACK_STATE` typing to include optional playback timing for
  resume persistence.
- Extended production config verification and release gating to require GSAV web
  URL, catalog URL, Supabase URL, and Supabase anon key values.
- Strengthened the release workflow identity order: the workflow now freezes the
  payload candidate before production evidence, records
  `release-evidence/release-candidate.txt`, writes `RELEASE_CANDIDATE_SHA` into
  the dry-run summary, verifies readiness before publish, and creates the GitHub
  release with `--target` set to the verified candidate SHA. The
  production-config and release-evidence-bundle verifiers now reject the old
  post-readiness mutation pattern.
- Tightened release evidence identity again: production preflight and runtime
  smoke now run after release-candidate identity capture, and
  `verify:release-evidence-bundle` rejects `gsav-preflight.json` or
  `gsav-runtime-smoke.json` when `diveoCommit` does not match
  `releaseCandidateSha`.
- Promoted generated APK version metadata capture to the first-class
  `npm run android:version-metadata` command and updated release CI plus the
  production-config verifier so the fallback metadata capture cannot drift back
  to an ad hoc script invocation.
- Added tested aggregate verification bundles:
  `npm run verify:local` for the WP-3 local quality sequence and
  `npm run verify:release-candidate` for local release-safety checks. Quality CI
  now runs `verify:local`, and the production-config verifier rejects quality
  workflows that drop that bundle.
- Tightened `npm run verify:release-candidate` so the expected-fail production
  config step must parse the production-config JSON, emit exactly the four
  missing-production-env errors with no unexpected errors, and report
  `qualityVerifiesLocalBundle`; the HTTPS-placeholder positive step must emit
  pass, placeholder GSAV URL, generated APK metadata capture,
  `capturesGsavHostIdentity`, and `qualityVerifiesLocalBundle` markers.
- Tightened release dry-run evidence so `verify:release-evidence-bundle` now
  requires `workflow_dispatch`, `publish_release=false`, no-publish proof,
  concrete GSAV host/build identity in both `gsav-preflight.json` and
  `gsav-runtime-smoke.json`, and candidate-commit matching. The release
  workflow now passes `GSAV_HOSTING_COMMIT` into both evidence-producing
  commands.
- Tightened release evidence again after the three-agent V&V review:
  `gsav-preflight.json` now records `requestRange=bytes=0-0`,
  `dry-run-summary.json` records app version, package version, Android
  `versionCode`, range probe URL, and range request, and
  `verify:release-evidence-bundle` compares those fields against
  `release-candidate.txt` and generated version metadata.
- Added an evidence-date guard to `verify:release-readiness`: completed rows
  must use real ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` dates and cannot be future-dated relative to
  the readiness run.
- Reworked the active QA templates so device, negative, artifact, generated
  version, installed APK smoke, dry-run, and scoped-exception rows use parser
  labels that `verify:release-readiness` actually accepts.
- Partially remediated the release-pipeline blocker: the current workflow now
  passes `RELEASE_EVIDENCE_MODE` to `verify:release-evidence-bundle`, and the
  verifier has separate `dry-run` and `publish` modes. Freezing the version-bump
  candidate as a committed branch or PR before production evidence collection
  remains pending before real publish signoff.
- Added the detailed payload-candidate/evidence-signoff implementation plan for
  the remaining candidate-freeze remediation. The plan now calls out the
  self-referential commit problem, required workflow changes, readiness-verifier
  coverage, allowed docs/evidence-only signoff diff, and publish validation
  handoff.
- Implemented the local payload-candidate/evidence-signoff remediation:
  `.github/workflows/release.yml` now requires `candidate_ref` for manual
  release runs, records the fixed payload SHA, rejects signoff diffs outside
  QA/audit/evidence files, removes release-time version bump, `git commit`, and
  `git push` steps, and passes `RELEASE_CANDIDATE_SHA` into preflight, runtime
  smoke, summary, and readiness. `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` now
  compares completed evidence rows to the payload SHA when provided and rejects
  non-evidence signoff diffs. `scripts/evidence-metadata.js` records the
  payload SHA in raw preflight/runtime evidence when `RELEASE_CANDIDATE_SHA` is
  set. `scripts/verify-native-production-config.js` now enforces the frozen
  candidate workflow shape.
- Tightened publish-readiness evidence ownership and durability: completed
  publish rows must name an owner, and final readiness rejects local
  `docs/qa-evidence/...` files or `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` when they are
  untracked or dirty.
- Tightened publish-readiness QA schema validation: `verify:release-readiness`
  now rejects `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` if the Evidence Log header drifts from
  the enforced Date, Platform, Device/Emulator, GSAV web URL, route, Result,
  Evidence path, and Notes columns.
- Strengthened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` again so an otherwise
  complete readiness pass is rejected when `app.json`, `package.json`, or
  `package-lock.json` has uncommitted changes. This closes the remaining
  synthetic identity gap where row text could match disk versions that were not
  part of `git HEAD`.
- Added coverage to the reusable GitHub Actions quality gate.
- Made this validation audit trackable by unignoring it in `.gitignore`.
- Started the native product feature-folder migration by moving feed, search,
  and creator hooks into `features/catalog/`, and the follow hook into
  `features/social/`; temporary GSAV hook compatibility wrappers were later
  removed.
- Moved scene card UI out of `components/` and later into `features/scene/`.
  `ContinueWatchingPill` moved to `features/player/`, and `Brand` moved to
  `shared/ui/`; temporary `components/*` wrappers were later removed.
- Moved the settings route implementation and settings store into
  `features/settings/`, the APK update hook into `features/app-update/`, and
  auth/saved-scenes stores into `features/social/`; temporary
  `hooks/useCheckUpdate.ts` and selected `store/*` compatibility exports were
  later removed.
- Moved the remaining route screen bodies out of `app/`:
  `features/catalog/HomeScreen.tsx`, `features/catalog/SearchScreen.tsx`,
  `features/catalog/CreatorScreen.tsx`, `features/social/LoginScreen.tsx`, and
  `features/social/LibraryScreen.tsx`. The corresponding Expo route files are
  now export stubs.
- Split `shared/theme.ts` into pure shared tokens, then moved app-wide theme
  access into `features/preferences/useTheme.ts`. Updated shared `Brand` so it
  receives its color from callers instead of importing app theme state.
- Moved platform-specific cache helpers and tests from `utils/cache*` to
  `features/settings/cache*`.
- Removed the obsolete `utils/gsavBridge.ts` compatibility barrel. Bridge helper
  tests now live at `features/player/bridge.test.ts` and import the helper-only
  player index. `package.json` lint scope no longer includes the deleted barrel.
- Added `scripts/gsav-native-runtime-smoke.js` and `npm run gsav:runtime-smoke`
  for JS-capable hosted embed validation through Playwright.
- Extracted saved-scene UI state out of `SceneCard` into
  `features/social/SaveSceneButton.tsx`. `SceneCard` is now presentational for
  save state and accepts a `thumbnailAccessory` supplied by feature screens.
- Removed Bilibili/DASH/proxy-era remnants that had no active GSAV-native
  imports: `dev-proxy.js`, `utils/buildMpd.ts`, `hooks/useAutoHideTimer.ts`,
  `shims/react-native-static-server.web.ts`, `npm run proxy`,
  `react-native-video`, `@dr.pogodin/react-native-static-server`, `pako`, and
  `@types/pako`. Also removed the `react-native-video` Expo plugin and the
  static-server Metro web shim alias, then refreshed `package-lock.json`.
- Added `scripts/verify-release-artifact.js`,
  `scripts/verify-release-artifact.test.mjs`, and
  `npm run verify:release-artifact`. The release workflow now runs it after the
  Android release build.
- Hardened release artifact verification to require the production GSAV web URL,
  catalog URL, Supabase URL, and public Supabase anon key in the APK bundle, to
  reject local/private/legacy bundle markers, and to fail if the merged Android
  manifest text is unavailable or enables cleartext traffic.
- Added APK SHA256 output to `npm run verify:release-artifact` so release
  artifact evidence can cite a checksum produced by the verifier instead of a
  separate manual hash step.
- Updated release CI to run `npm run gsav:preflight` against the production GSAV
  URL with local asset range checks skipped, and to pass the Gradle merged
  manifest path into the artifact verifier.
- Tightened native production config verification so legacy URL schemes fail and
  the release artifact verifier must remain exposed in `package.json`.
- Added source-level Android version metadata verification. `app.json` now
  carries `android.versionCode`, `scripts/bump-version.js` updates it before
  Expo prebuild, release CI re-runs production config verification after the
  local version bump, and verifier tests reject version/versionCode drift.
- Expanded `npm run lint` to cover the full `app/` tree. This caught a root
  bootstrap effect dependency warning, fixed by making the app-update hook
  callbacks stable and listing the real effect dependencies.
- Removed contiguous local loopback literals and legacy source-system comments
  from active runtime files so release artifact string scans are less likely to
  false-fail on dev fallback text.
- Updated README and architecture docs to reflect the current feature split and
  removed compatibility bridge ownership.
- Used three parallel review agents to assess architecture elegance,
  verification/validation coverage, and release/legacy readiness. Their findings
  were incorporated into `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` as a target
  dependency matrix, a new dependency-graph refinement phase, expanded QA rows,
  and stricter release gates.
- Created `features/app-shell/RootLayout.tsx` for root bootstrap, Sentry, font
  loading, providers, startup hydration, update checks, and route-stack
  composition. `app/_layout.tsx` is now a thin Expo Router adapter.
- Created `features/preferences/` for app-wide preference state and theme access.
  Removed `features/settings/settingsStore.ts`, `features/settings/useTheme.ts`,
  and `features/player/theme.ts`; `shared/theme.ts` now owns the GSAV accent
  tokens. Active features now import preference state from
  `features/preferences/` and design tokens from `shared/theme.ts`.
- Moved saved-library scene resolution out of `services/gsav.ts`; it now lives
  in `features/scene/loadScenesByBackendIds.ts`, with a feature-local test.
  `services/` stays closer to client construction, env config, and shared GSAV
  package re-exports.
- Added `features/catalog/catalogAdapter.ts` as the feature-owned catalog access
  layer over `services/gsav`. Feed/search/creator hooks, catalog display types,
  and social saved-scene resolution now use the adapter instead of direct
  `services/gsav` imports, except for the adapter itself.
- Added `scripts/verify-import-boundaries.js`,
  `scripts/verify-import-boundaries.test.mjs`, and
  `npm run verify:import-boundaries`. The reusable quality workflow now runs the
  import-boundary audit after coverage. The audit enforces the documented
  app/feature/shared/service/utils dependency matrix while allowing only the
  current named temporary cross-feature exceptions.
- Finished unused direct-dependency cleanup for packages with no active imports:
  `axios`, `expo-clipboard`, `expo-linear-gradient`, `expo-media-library`,
  `expo-network`, `react-native-pager-view`, and `react-native-qrcode-svg`.
  Removed stale Metro web aliases and deleted the now-unused web shims for
  clipboard, network, and pager-view modules.
- Folded the latest three-agent review into Phase 8: shrink cross-feature
  import allowlists, extract app-shell bootstrap structure, decouple catalog
  cards from player URL construction, and document the remaining route-layout
  exception. Also removed stale cache wording that referenced legacy MPD/QR
  artifacts.
- Hardened release metadata checks after review: `scripts/bump-version.js` now
  updates `package-lock.json`, production config verification rejects
  app/package/lockfile version drift, release artifact verification rejects
  non-HTTPS baked production URLs, and release CI commits the lockfile after a
  verified version bump.
- Added a configurable production `.gsav` range probe to
  `scripts/gsav-native-preflight.js`; release CI now requires
  `GSAV_RANGE_PROBE_URL` and validates a `206` byte-range response before the
  Android build.
- Refactored `scripts/gsav-native-preflight.js` behind exported testable
  functions and added unit coverage for route shell checks, required production
  range probes, missing range-probe failures, and optional CORS enforcement.
- Replaced the artifact verifier's external `unzip` dependency with direct
  APK/ZIP bundle extraction for `assets/*.bundle`, with an APK-style fixture
  test covering the extraction path.
- Added `vitest.config.mjs` with conservative global coverage thresholds so
  `npm run test:coverage` now fails on regressions instead of acting only as a
  report. The lint gate now includes the Vitest config file.
- Completed the next Phase 8 structure cleanup: `RootLayout` now delegates
  startup effects to `features/app-shell/useAppBootstrap.ts`, route stack
  declarations to `features/app-shell/AppStack.tsx`, and
  `app/creator/_layout.tsx` to `features/app-shell/CreatorLayout.tsx`.
- Decoupled `SceneCard` from player URL construction. Long-press behavior is
  passed in by screen composition, and share URL construction lives in
  `features/scene/sceneShare.ts` with focused tests.
- Moved generic Expo Router param normalization from `features/player/routes.ts`
  to `shared/routeParams.ts`, removing a non-player catalog dependency.
- Added `features/social/authSession.ts` as the public cross-feature auth
  contract. Player, settings, and app-shell now consume auth state through that
  contract instead of importing `gsavAuthStore`; the import-boundary verifier now
  rejects direct cross-feature auth-store imports.
- Added `features/preferences/preferenceAccess.ts` and
  `features/social/savedSceneAccess.ts` as public contracts for cross-feature
  preference and saved-scene bootstrap access. Player/settings/app-shell no
  longer import preference or saved-scenes stores directly, and the boundary
  verifier now rejects those cross-feature store imports while allowing internal
  feature use.
- Added `features/app-update/updateAccess.ts` as the public cross-feature update
  contract. App-shell now uses a startup update-check contract, settings uses a
  settings update-status contract, and the boundary verifier rejects direct
  cross-feature imports of `features/app-update/useCheckUpdate`.
- Removed unused dev dependency `express`, leaving the full and production audit
  result at the same transitive Expo/React Native/tooling advisory count.
- Folded the latest three-agent elegance review into
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`: the detailed remaining plan now
  calls out remaining catalog/social composition, feature barrel policy, `/explore`
  ownership, bridge protocol ownership, stricter service-import hygiene, and
  release-candidate validation gates.
- Added `features/scene/` as the shared scene presentation/domain surface.
  `SceneCard`, scene sharing, and scene item typing live there. Catalog and
  social screens consume `features/scene`, social no longer imports catalog
  scene UI/adapters, and the boundary verifier rejects social-to-catalog scene
  imports.
- Tightened `scripts/verify-import-boundaries.js` so feature screens and layouts
  cannot import `services/*` directly. Service access remains allowed from
  adapters, stores, and domain/access helpers such as catalog adapters and
  social-owned saved-scene lookup helpers.
- Trimmed feature barrels to match the enforced boundary model. Catalog and
  social indexes no longer export route screens, the unused settings index was
  removed, and the import-boundary tests reject app routes importing feature
  barrels instead of explicit screen roots.
- Added release workflow `workflow_dispatch` support for a non-publishing
  release dry run. Manual runs default to `publish_release=false`, upload the
  verified APK/manifest evidence artifact, and only commit the version bump or
  create a GitHub release when running on `master` push or with
  `publish_release=true`.
- Extended `scripts/verify-native-production-config.js` to reject removal of
  the manual release dry-run trigger, publish gate, or evidence artifact upload.
- Ran a patch-level dependency refresh within the current package ranges. The
  raw audit result dropped from 21 advisories (`1 low`, `15 moderate`, `4 high`,
  `1 critical`) to 10 moderate advisories, with no high or critical findings.
- Added `scripts/verify-dependency-audit.js`,
  `scripts/verify-dependency-audit.test.mjs`, and
  `npm run verify:dependency-audit`. The verifier accepts only the known
  Expo/Sentry tooling-chain moderate advisory set, records owner
  `native release owner` with revisit date `2026-07-31`, and fails on any
  high/critical, unexpected package, unexpected direct advisory, or expired
  accepted-risk revisit date.
- Added the dependency audit disposition step to `.github/workflows/quality.yml`,
  so PR and release quality gates fail if the accepted advisory set changes.
- Updated the dependency-audit verifier's Windows subprocess invocation so
  `npm run verify:dependency-audit` no longer emits Node's shell-args
  deprecation warning.
- Tightened `scripts/verify-import-boundaries.js` so repository-root absolute
  imports such as `features/...`, `shared/...`, `services/...`, `app/...`, and
  `utils/...` are resolved and checked by the same dependency-matrix rules as
  relative imports.
- Replaced the legacy mojibake pull request template with a GSAV-native template
  that asks for typecheck, lint, dependency audit, import-boundary, unit,
  coverage, config verifier, preflight/runtime smoke, device QA, and release
  evidence when applicable.
- Extracted native progress bridge persistence decisions into
  `features/player/progressBridge.ts`. `GsavWebView` now delegates bridge
  progress handling to that helper, with focused tests for `GSAV_ENDED` clearing
  resume state, `GSAV_PLAYBACK_STATE` saving immediately, and high-frequency
  `GSAV_FRAME` throttling.
- Added `features/player/gsavProgressStore.test.ts` to cover persisted resume
  hydration, invalid/early progress rejection, explicit clear, and near-finished
  playback clearing stored resume state.
- Added `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js`,
  `scripts/verify-release-readiness.test.mjs`, and
  `npm run verify:release-readiness`. The verifier reads
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` and fails publish readiness until Android/iOS route
  QA, negative QA, production `.gsav` range probe, release APK/manifest scan,
  generated versionCode metadata, and non-publishing release dry-run evidence
  rows are complete with durable evidence paths. Local `docs/qa-evidence/...`
  entries must exist as tracked, committed files; otherwise the row must link to
  a trusted GitHub CI/artifact/release URL under `opsiclear/diveo` or
  `opsiclear/gsav-hosting`.
- Wired `npm run verify:release-readiness` into the release workflow only for
  publish paths (`master` push or manual `publish_release=true`), after the
  APK/manifest evidence artifact is uploaded and before any GitHub release is
  created. The default `publish_release=false` dry run can still run to generate
  evidence.
- Extended `scripts/verify-native-production-config.js` and its tests so
  removal of the `verify:release-readiness` package script or release workflow
  gate is rejected.
- Added `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js`, `scripts/verify-doc-drift.test.mjs`, and
  `npm run verify:docs-drift`. The verifier checks active README,
  contributing, PR-template, architecture, QA, and ADR 0002 docs for the
  current native-product plus embedded-player ownership markers, current quality
  commands, and stale compatibility/legacy path markers.
- Wired `npm run verify:docs-drift` into the reusable quality workflow so PR and
  release quality gates reject drift in active contributor-facing docs.
- Added a current release-blocker table to this audit, covering Android/iOS QA,
  production range probe, real APK/manifest scan, generated version metadata,
  release dry run, and publish-readiness status.
- Expanded `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` with an ordered execution
  plan, explicit evidence order, detailed negative validation triggers,
  `/explore` and bridge-protocol decision gates, catalog/social/player allowlist
  cleanup, scene-share decoupling, boundary-parser hardening, and generated
  versionCode validation.
- Expanded `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` with per-route validation steps and
  negative-case trigger/pass/evidence guidance so pending evidence rows are
  reproducible.
- Updated the PR template, contributing guide, and architecture doc so docs
  drift, runtime smoke, release-readiness, generated versionCode evidence, and
  current architecture follow-ups are visible in contributor-facing workflows.
- Tightened `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so those new contributor-doc markers
  are required and stale completed-work follow-ups are rejected.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-import-boundaries.js` so CommonJS `require(...)`,
  dynamic `import(...)`, and relative imports that escape active source roots are
  checked by the same dependency-matrix rules as static imports.
- Added unit coverage for the native `/gsav/:id?t=` alias path by extracting
  `buildGsavScreenWatchPath`. The test proves `/gsav/test?t=2.5` resolves to
  `/watch/test?t=2.5` before `embed=native` is applied by the WebView URL
  builder.
- Added a production-config placeholder audit to `.github/workflows/quality.yml`
  and extended `scripts/verify-native-production-config.js` so removal of that
  reusable CI gate fails local/release verification.
- Moved pure GSAV watch-path and web-origin helpers to `shared/gsavRoutes.ts`
  and `shared/gsavWeb.ts`. A later follow-up removed the temporary
  `features/player/url.ts` compatibility export, so player callers now import
  `shared/gsavWeb.ts` directly and `features/scene/sceneShare.ts` depends on
  shared route/config contracts instead of player internals.
- Removed the scene-to-player route/config allowlist from
  `scripts/verify-import-boundaries.js` and shrank catalog-to-player access to
  the documented `ContinueWatchingPill` product-screen composition point. Added
  regression tests that reject scene-to-player route imports and catalog imports
  from player route internals.
- Resolved the `/explore` ownership decision. ADR 0002, the implementation
  plan, QA docs, and architecture docs now record `/explore` as an intentional
  hosted runtime route owned through `features/player/ExploreScreen.tsx`, while
  native Home/Search/Creator/Library remain the catalog discovery surfaces.
  `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` now requires that decision marker in active
  docs, and the import-boundary tests cover the explicit `app/explore.tsx`
  route-stub exception.
- Split generated Gradle/APK `versionCode` validation into its own
  release-readiness evidence row. `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` now
  checks 26 release-blocking rows and requires the generated version metadata row
  result to mention `versionCode`, instead of relying on the broader APK artifact
  row to imply that check.
- Added a detailed work-package plan to
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`, including strict publish ceremony,
  route-stub map, feature contract ledger, bridge protocol subplan, current
  evidence requirements, and explicit verification/validation exit gates.
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_QA.md` now requires publish-candidate identity,
  both-platform negative validation evidence or named exceptions, and artifact
  verifier/checksum details before evidence can support release signoff.
- Refined the detailed implementation plan after the latest three-agent review:
  added top-level structural acceptance criteria, active structural decisions,
  explicit bridge ownership, service-access stack rules, reproducible QA
  evidence fields, device log-capture guidance, exact negative-case trigger
  methods, accepted APK metadata sources, and clearer release dry-run/range
  probe acceptance.
- Strengthened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so completed QA rows must
  include publish-candidate identity, route or negative-case observed signals,
  both Android and iOS negative-case evidence or a named owner/revisit exception,
  release artifact scan details, generated `versionCode` metadata, production
  range-probe `206`, dry-run markers, and checksum/artifact details where
  applicable. Added regression tests for missing candidate identity, combined
  negative-case coverage, and insufficient artifact scan detail.
- Strengthened `scripts/verify-dependency-audit.js` so the accepted Expo/Sentry
  tooling-chain advisory disposition fails automatically after
  `2026-07-31`. Added tests proving the disposition is valid through that date
  and rejected starting `2026-08-01`.
- Strengthened `scripts/verify-doc-drift.js` so it also checks
  `docs/GSAV_NATIVE_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` and
  `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_VALIDATION_AUDIT.md` for the current work-package,
  evidence-detail, release-readiness, and dependency-audit control markers. The
  implementation plan and audit are allowed to contain historical removed-path
  references, while active user-facing docs still reject stale markers.
- Moved canonical native bridge event/command/version protocol ownership into
  `@opsiclear/gsav-bridge`. The sibling `../gsavjs/packages/gsav-bridge`
  package now exports session, native command, bridge readiness, route,
  capability, playback, frame, error, and version wire contracts plus the native
  command envelope builder. The updated package was built, packed into
  `vendor/opsiclear-gsav-bridge-0.1.0.tgz`, installed into diveo, and consumed
  through `features/player/bridgeTypes.ts`. Diveo keeps local bridge reducers,
  progress persistence, origin gates, injected script composition, and WebView
  lifecycle logic.
- Enforced the external GSAV package import policy in
  `scripts/verify-import-boundaries.js`. Direct imports from
  `@opsiclear/gsav-bridge` are limited to the player bridge facade/session
  helper and runtime-smoke bridge helper. Direct imports from
  `@opsiclear/gsav-client` are limited to `services/gsav` and
  `features/social/socialAdapter.ts`. The verifier output now prints the documented
  external package allowlist, and focused tests reject unauthorized catalog,
  social-screen, or bridge-package imports.
- Added `scripts/write-release-evidence-summary.js` and wired it into the
  release workflow before artifact upload. The workflow now writes
  `release-evidence/dry-run-summary.json` and
  `release-evidence/evidence-checksums.txt`, with APK, merged manifest, command
  evidence, run URL, artifact name, publish/no-publish state, and SHA256 values
  summarized for dry-run evidence. `verify:native-production-config` now rejects
  release workflows that omit that summary or checksum manifest.
- Added `scripts/capture-android-version-metadata.js` and wired it into the
  release workflow in place of the inline `aapt`-only shell block. Release CI
  now writes `release-evidence/apk-version-metadata.txt` after trying
  `aapt dump badging`, `apkanalyzer manifest print`, then
  `bundletool dump manifest`. `verify:native-production-config` now rejects
  release workflows that omit the fallback capture script or metadata evidence
  file, and `verify:release-readiness` accepts generated APK metadata from those
  sources without requiring an exception.
- Added auth-state-aware session bridge helpers in
  `features/player/sessionBridge.ts`. Native WebView and Expo web iframe embeds
  now build no session message/script before auth initialization completes, so a
  transient `GSAV_CLEAR_SESSION` cannot be sent while stored auth is still
  restoring.
- Expanded bridge helper tests to cover the auth-initialization negative case:
  no pre-init session message, post-init clear-session for signed-out users, and
  post-init set-session for signed-in users. Device evidence for the same
  timeline remains required before release signoff.
- Extracted session bridge side effects into auth-state-aware host helpers.
  Native WebView script injection and Expo web iframe `postMessage` now both
  call those helpers, and source-level regression tests guard against the hosts
  bypassing them in favor of raw session message/script builders.
- Hardened `npm run gsav:runtime-smoke` so each route must emit a compatible
  `GSAV_BRIDGE_READY` version range. The command now records native bridge
  version metadata and per-route `bridgeReady` summaries in its JSON evidence.
  Release-readiness detail checks now reject runtime-smoke rows that omit a
  bridge-version compatibility signal.
- Hardened production range-probe release-readiness checks so a completed row
  must include the `GSAV_RANGE_PROBE_URL` or `.gsav` URL, `Range: bytes=0-0`,
  `206`, `Content-Range`, and release workflow/preflight context. A row that
  only says the probe returned `206` no longer satisfies publish readiness.
- Hardened non-publishing release dry-run checks so a completed row must include
  explicit proof that `publish_release=false` produced no GitHub release and no
  publish side effect, in addition to the workflow run URL, production preflight,
  APK verifier, evidence upload, artifact name, and checksum details.
- Ran a fresh three-agent review focused on architecture elegance, release
  verifier gaps, and QA validation structure. The current React Native/web split
  remains sound; the actionable gaps were stricter catalog/social/player
  composition exit criteria, exact release evidence fields, mixed local/URL
  evidence validation, and the `Range: bytes=0-0` mismatch between docs and
  preflight behavior.
- Hardened `scripts/verify-release-readiness.js` so the Android release APK
  artifact row now has to summarize `verify:release-artifact`/APK verifier
  output, production GSAV web/catalog/Supabase/anon-key presence, APK path,
  merged manifest path, cleartext disabled, local/private marker absence,
  legacy Bilibili/proxy/DASH marker absence, and checksum details. It also now
  checks local `docs/qa-evidence/...` paths even when the same row includes a
  durable CI/artifact URL.
- Hardened publish-candidate identity checks so completed QA rows must state
  the `package.json` version as well as the diveo commit, app version, and
  Android `versionCode` where relevant. The durable JS runtime-smoke QA row was
  updated with the current package version so it remains accepted by
  `npm run verify:release-readiness`.
- Hardened release-readiness exception handling so exception rows now need a
  named owner and an unexpired ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` revisit date. Weak exception
  text and expired revisit dates no longer suppress Android/iOS negative
  evidence requirements.
- Hardened production range-probe and dry-run evidence checks. Range-probe rows
  now need a non-local HTTPS `.gsav` URL, `Range: bytes=0-0`, `206`,
  `Content-Range`, and release workflow/preflight context. Dry-run rows now
  need `workflow_dispatch`/manual-run, production-secret, Android build,
  production preflight, APK verifier, evidence upload, artifact name, checksum,
  and no-publish-side-effect markers.
- Updated the release workflow to capture durable command outputs under
